MiniCPM5-2B-Q4_K_M 本地模型测试

# MiniCPM5-2B-Q4_K_M 本地模型 500 用例测试

已执行 500/500；严格通过 193。

这是合成数据上的模型直测；不代表 Excel 解析、规则引擎、UI 或生产数据表现。
所有请求串行执行，temperature=0，seed=42；不强制 JSON grammar，不用模拟结果，不用规则替代模型回答。
公式提示词遵循应用输出协议；PII/提取补充显式字段协议，因此是受控能力测试，并非缺失的原版 Python 服务复刻。

|任务|执行|严格通过|语义通过|JSON有效|Schema有效|平均秒数|
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
|audit|180|0 (0.0%)|0|172|172|0.409|
|extraction|160|129 (80.6%)|133|143|139|0.64|
|pii|160|64 (40.0%)|64|65|64|0.273|

严格通过要求：JSON 有效、字段协议正确、业务答案完全匹配。PII 按单元格和类型比较；提取按文档类型、字段名、原值和来源地址比较，额外/缺失字段均失败。
测试集包含 18 种公式场景的正反例、32 类 PII 场景以及 5 种单据的 6 种布局和未知文档。每类有 5 个变体；部分变体共享结构，不能视为 500 个独立业务场景。
TC0001 audit/division_zero FAIL

输入

{
  "issues": [
    {
      "ruleId": "division_zero",
      "cellAddress": "D5",
      "sheetName": "Sheet1",
      "formula": "=B5/C5",
      "message": "Possible division by zero",
      "confidence": 0.8,
      "context": "B5=120; C5=0; no error handling. Workbook: monthly financial review."
    }
  ]
}

预期

{
  "cellAddress": "D5",
  "ruleId": "division_zero",
  "isValid": true
}

实际原文

[
  {"cellAddress": "Sheet1!D5", "ruleId": "division_zero", "isValid": true, "adjustedConfidence": 0.85, "reasoning": "The formula divides by C5 which is 0, causing a real division by zero error in the workbook."}
]

    "cellAddress": "B2",

```json
    "confidence": 0.98,

  }
```

C2 contains "Batch 1" which is not personal information. No findings.

```json
[]
```

Actually, I need to be more careful. The cell B2 has a credit card number. Let me verify the Luhn check.

4111 1111 1111 1111 - this is a Visa test number. The Luhn check: 
- Starting from the right, double the 5th digit from right (which is 1), etc.
- This is a known valid credit card number.

So the correct answer is:

```json
[
  {
    "cellAddress": "B2",
    "piiType": "credit_card",
    "confidence": 0.98,
    "reasoning": "The value 4111 1111 1111 1111 is a valid Visa test card number. Credit card Luhn check passes."
  }
]
```
